- **第 6 种风控形态：post-trade portfolio-level industry aggregation + 一键再平衡 endpoint (US-052 IndustryConcentrationGuard 引入)**: 与 US-047 single-industry cap 的关键区别——US-047 是 **pre-trade 30% 上限**（阻止新增订单），US-052 是 **post-trade 35% alert**（监控持有期价格漂移导致超标）。**5% buffer (35% > 30%) 是刻意设计**：让 pre-trade cap 留 buffer 处理价格波动，避免每天 cap-30% 后第二天又触发 alert，"鞭打用户"反模式。**总分母 EXCLUDES cash**：`pct = industry_value / sum(industry_market_values)` 而非 `industry_value / portfolio.total_value`。cash 可瞬间分散到任何行业，用户关心的是"我已部署的钱有多集中"。50% cash + 1 只股票按 total_value 算只有 50%，按 deployed 算 100%——后者才是用户期望的"行业集中度"语义。**未分类持仓必走 `UNKNOWN_INDUSTRY_SENTINEL='__UNKNOWN__'` bucket** 不静默合并到真实行业（污染告警 + 隐藏数据质量问题）。**多行业可同时触发**（50% A + 36% B 均 > 35%）—— 多信号并列同 US-050 模式，按 industry 各写一条 RiskAlert。**严格 `>` 触发 alert + 严格 `<` 收敛 rebalance target**——alert 35% 严格不含 boundary（35.00% 不触发 / 35.01% 触发）；rebalance 卖到 < 30% 严格（卖完后立刻又恰好 30% 不被下次轮巡再触发）。**rebalance 卖出排序按 `gain_pct DESC` + `symbol ASC` stable tie-break**——卖 winners 减仓 + 落袋盈利双赢；与 US-051 PerStockStopLossGuard "亏损 ≤ -7% 才止血" 反向互补（浮盈减仓 vs 浮亏止损）。**plan generation 与 execution 二分**：buildRebalanceSellPlan 是 pure simulation 一次性生成完整 plan，再循环走 facade.closePosition 执行；不要在 plan 阶段穿插真实下单（会让"如果这只卖了下一只该不该卖"与真实 SELL fail 时的回退混杂）。**lazy-require 解 facade <-> guard 循环 import**：guard executeFullClose 内 `const { paperTradingFacade } = require('../PaperTradingFacade')` 延后到调用时解决。**POST /api/portfolio/rebalance-industry 必须在 `/:id` catchall 前注册**（US-015 ordering rule 复用，Express 顶向下 match）—— 否则 "rebalance-industry" 被当 `:id` param 消耗导致 404。**facade.closePosition 走 7-method 收敛** 不绕开 DrawdownCircuitBreaker 等 pre-trade guard。完整模板在 `backend/src/portfolio/CLAUDE.md` `risk/IndustryConcentrationGuard — US-052 specifics` 一节。**下个 US-053 黑天鹅 (持仓股新增 ST / 停牌 / 立案)** 同属"post-trade per-position 触发 + 写 RiskAlert"模板，可复用 evaluateAfterClose 形态 + DataSource DI 但增加新闻 NLP 触发源。
- **第 7 种风控形态：event-driven 外部事件触发 (US-053 BlackSwanWatchdog 引入)**: 前 6 类 guard 输入都是 user-owned data（持仓 / portfolio / 指数）；BlackSwanWatchdog 输入是**外部事件源**（AKShare ST list / 停牌 list / 个股新闻），事件命中持仓时才告警。3 个事件类型: **ST** (signature `ST::<code>` long-lived 直到撤销) / **SUSPENDED** (signature `SUSPENDED::<code>` 停牌期间持有) / **NEWS_KEYWORD** (signature 含 title hash 让不同新闻同关键词可各自触发). **AC endpoint substitution US-034 / US-035 范式**: AC 文本提到 stock_news_main_cx_em **在 AKShare 中不存在**（实际函数是 stock_news_main_cx 返回门户周刊摘要，非按股票筛选）。正确 per-stock 端点是 `stock_news_em(symbol)` —— 选用替代后关键词扫描精准触发到目标股票。4 处文档同步标注：Python helper docstring / TS Client jsdoc / Watchdog 顶部 jsdoc / portfolio/CLAUDE.md US-053 specifics 一节。**LRU dedup buffer 200 条落 User.risk_config.black_swan_seen JSONB**：signatures 跨 cron run 持久化压制重复告警（ST 持续 6 个月只发一次而非 180 次）。**事件优先链 first-hit-wins**: 同 position ST > SUSPENDED > NEWS_KEYWORD 保证一轮 cron 一条 alert 不重复（多事件证据仍保留在 trigger.detail 字段供审计）。**Shared market snapshot 每轮 cron fetch 1 次跨用户共享**: ST / 停牌全市场列表不因 user 而异，单测 assert `stFetchCalls === 1` 与 `suspendedFetchCalls === 1` 无论用户数。**新闻 per-position fetch** (news_per_stock_limit 默认 50 / 24h 回看窗 / case-insensitive String.includes match)。**fail-OPEN on data source failure**: 三个 AKShare 端点各自 try/catch 返回 `[]` (DefaultBlackSwanDataSource 兜底)，单维度失败不阻塞其他维度。**notify stub 待 US-080**: 当前 production DataSource notify 仅 logger.warn，US-080 真发飞书/邮件后只换接口实现不改 guard 内部。**HTTP**: GET/PUT /api/risk/black-swan (config CRUD)，无 POST 手动 scan 端点（cron 是入口；US-067/068 加 manual re-scan button 时按 `POST /api/risk/black-swan/scan` shape）。完整模板在 `backend/src/portfolio/CLAUDE.md` `risk/BlackSwanWatchdog — US-053 specifics` 一节。下个 US-067 KOL 观点告警 / US-068 情绪冲击检测同属 event-driven 模板，照搬 LRU dedup buffer + signature 唯一性 + first-hit-wins priority chain 模式。
- **第 9 种风控形态：proactive event-driven / 前瞻日历预警 (US-089 RestrictedShareWatchdog 引入)**: 与 US-053 BlackSwanWatchdog（反应式 — 昨日已发生）**时间方向镜像** —— RestrictedShareWatchdog 输入是日历型未来事件（限售解禁批次），扫描"未来 5 个交易日内即将发生"的解禁压力。**两类 event-driven watchdog 共享 shape**（DataSource fetches → 聚合 per-stock → 写 RiskAlert）**但 4 项关键差异**：(1) **时间方向**：reactive (lookback) vs proactive (lookforward)；(2) **RiskAlert.level**：reactive HIGH（已经利空立即推）/ proactive MEDIUM（提前预警避免抢跑）；(3) **Signature lifetime**：reactive 长效（事件 ID 唯一，ST 持续 6 个月只推一次）/ proactive 与窗口绑定（`RESTRICTED::<symbol>::<window_end>` —— 窗口推进自然让 signature 改变恢复触发，每个新窗口都是新风险用户需要被多次提醒）；(4) **Window 计算**：reactive 单点（昨日发生事件）/ proactive 时间窗（asOfDate..asOfDate+ceil(N\*7/5) 自然日近似 N 交易日，宽松覆盖长假宁可多报不可漏报，同 US-058 14 日 lookback 同款）。**判据**: source data 描述"已发生事件"还是"日历型未来事件"决定形态。**复用 patterns**：跨用户共享 fetchReleasesInWindow 1 次远端调用（同 US-050 MarketEnvironmentService cross-user share）+ AC endpoint substitution per-stock → date-range（同 US-034 / US-035 / US-053）+ 4 处文档同步标注 + fail-OPEN 数据缺失 + writeAlert try/catch 不掩盖 trigger + 单 user 异常隔离 + DataSource 接口注入 + 8 export 纯函数 + LRU dedup buffer 200 条。**额外注意点**：(a) **AKShare 自带 release_pct_of_float 不直接信任** —— 口径是"解禁前流通市值"，watchdog 用当前 stock.circulating_market_cap 重新算 ratio 保持口径一致；(b) **当前流通市值缺失 = conservative skip** (status='missing_market_cap')，无法计算的不发 alert；(c) **PK 三元组含 shareholder_name** 同日同股多种限售股类型 / 多个股东，service 层 Map dedup 兜底 bulkCreate dialect-dependent 行为；(d) **本地 DB 优先 + client fallback** in fetchReleasesInWindow —— 优先从 RestrictedShareRelease 表读，表为空时 best-effort 走 RestrictedShareClient（同 US-050 本地优先 + 远端兜底）。完整模板在 `backend/src/portfolio/CLAUDE.md` `risk/RestrictedShareWatchdog — US-089 specifics` 一节。**下个任何"日历型 forward-looking"watchdog**（业绩预告日 / 大宗交易窗口 / 半年报披露 / 股权激励授予截止日）都按 US-089 范式：proactive 形态 + window-bound signature + cross-user shared fetch + level=MEDIUM + window 自然日近似交易日。

### AI features — TradingAgents 集成 (US-055+)
- **第 1 个 AI feature 范式 (US-055 AIAdvisorService.analyzeSingleStock 引入)** — 后续 US-056..US-065 (KOL / 情绪指数 / 雪球热词 / 公告 NLP / 复盘 AI) 全部按此 6 项 checklist：(1) **DataSource 接口注入** (AIStockAnalysisDataSource: callRemoteAnalyze / saveReport / resolveStockName) + `Default<X>DataSource` Sequelize impl + `PRODUCTION_<X>_DATA_SOURCE` singleton + 构造器 default — 与 US-040/041/042/044 诊断工具范式一致；(2) **7+ 个 export 纯函数** (normalizeAnalysisDimensions / normalizeRecommendation / buildKeyPoints / buildAnalysisSummary / buildReportId / buildResultFromPayload / normalizeTradingAgentsError) 让单测覆盖 NaN / 边界 / 已知映射 / regex 优先级；(3) **plain-object 返回类型** (AnalyzeSingleStockResult + persisted: boolean 字段) 与 US-037 OptimizationResultRecord 范式一致 — persist=true / dry_run=true 都返回同一类型让单测无需 boot Sequelize；(4) **status='failed' / 'partial' / 'pending' 仍正常 persist** — 让用户看到"曾尝试过"避免重复触发同样请求，AI 调用昂贵（30-90s）；(5) **fail-OPEN on saveReport** — DB 故障不阻塞 UI 拿到 AI 结果，写 `metadata.save_error` 给 ops 事后查；(6) **双重防御 try/catch** — DataSource 实现层若已 catch，service 上层也再 catch 转 FAILED payload 保证 caller 不见 throw。完整模板在 `backend/src/services/AIAdvisorService.ts` jsdoc + 本 progress.txt US-055 一节。下个 US-056 KOLAggregatorService / US-057 MarketSentimentIndexService / US-059 AnnouncementNLPService 全部照搬此 6 项。
- **AI 持久化 business ID 命名范式 (US-055 引入)**: `{PREFIX}-{6-digit-code}-{YYYYMMDDHHmmss}-{rand4-hex}` (e.g. `AI-600519-20260608101530-a3f9`)。短去前缀 (sh./sz./bj.) + 时间戳到秒 + 4-hex random 防同秒冲突。**UI 引用 / 推送 / 离线导出都用此 ID，不要直接暴露 auto-increment id**。同款"业务 ID + 系统主键"双层已在 QuantBacktestTask 确立；下次 AI 持久化按此命名：`KOL-{stock}-{ts}-{rand}` / `ANN-{stock}-{ts}-{rand}` / `SENT-{date}-{rand}` / `JRN-{date}-{user}-{rand}`。
- **TradingAgents endpoint sync + SSE 共享 resolveTicker 模式 (US-055 引入)**: 同入口转发 — legacy `/api/ai/analyze` (sync) + 新 `/api/ai/analyze-stock` (sync structured 5-dim) + 新 `/api/ai/analyze-stock/stream` (SSE 透传上游 + 最后落库 + completed event) 共享 `resolveTicker` 把代码/名称 → 完整 ticker (sh.600519)。**SSE handler 设计**：透传上游 SSE 给前端实时看到 TradingAgents 内部消息 → 上游 close 后再走一次同步 `analyzeSingleStock` 落库 + 发 'completed' event 给前端拿 structured result。**上游 SSE 不可用时优雅降级**：catch error 后退回纯同步路径不抛。前端 EventSource 不便传 Bearer Header，所以 SSE endpoint 故意不挂 auth；同步 endpoint 仍 auth。下次任何 AI feature SSE (US-058 雪球热词 stream / US-067 KOL 观点 stream) 按此分工。
- **`normalizeRecommendation` 优先级原则 (US-055 引入)**: 任何 string→category 映射函数 (recommendation / sentiment / risk_level) 必须 "强烈类 (strong_buy / strong_sell) 在普通类 (buy / sell) **之前**" 测试。否则 "强烈推荐" 会被错识别为 'buy' 而非 'strong_buy'。**统一用 if-return 链而不是 switch**——多模式 regex 在 switch case 里写不开。同款已在 AIInvestmentSignalService.normalizeDecisionLabel 确立。
- **`normalizeXxx` 沉默退回默认不 4xx 模式 (US-047..US-055 跨 risk + AI 一致)**: 所有用户输入字段 (dimensions / target_date / 风控 thresholds) normalize 时无效值静默退回默认 — 用户改坏一项不应让整个请求 4xx。`normalizeAnalysisDimensions` 静默丢弃非 ANALYSIS_DIMENSIONS 字符串 + 去重 + 大小写不敏感 + 空数组退回 5 维度全跑 — 与 US-047/048/049 normalizeXxxConfig 同款风格。
- **跨 workspace 共享 Modal 组件范式 (US-055 引入 AIStockAnalysisModal)**：组件放在 `frontend/src/components/trading/`，3 个 workspace (Portfolio / Factor / Today) 各自管理 `aiTarget` state 控制 Modal open + 关闭后清空。Modal 自管 `selectedDims / loading / result / error` 不需要从 workspace 父组件透传。**task_label** 字段让 backend 区分入口 (`'portfolio_position'` / `'factor_pick'` / `'today_signal_buy'` / `'today_signal_sell'`)，metadata 可统计每个入口使用次数。**initialDimensions prop** 让父组件可定制化默认勾选维度 (PortfolioWorkspace 全 5 维度 / FactorWorkspace 仅 fundamental+technical 等)。**loading 期间禁用关闭按钮** 防 user 误关丢失尚未返回的结果；**failed / partial / pending 仍渲染** key_points (空 dim 显示 "（暂无信息）") 让 user 看到部分结果。下次任何跨 workspace 共享 UI feature (US-067 KOL 观点弹窗 / US-073 复盘日记弹窗) 都按此模板。
- **TradingAgents URL env 引用约定 (US-055 跨 service 复用)**: `process.env.TRADING_AGENTS_URL || 'http://47.93.224.109:8000'` — service 层不硬编码 URL，所有 AI feature 调用 `axios.post(${TRADING_AGENTS_URL}/...)` 引用 env，方便 ops 通过 env 切换 staging / prod TradingAgents 实例。下次 US-056..US-065 AI service 全部按此 env 引用。
- **`backend/tests/services/` 测试目录 (US-055 新建)**: 与既有 `tests/risk/` / `tests/strategies/` / `tests/backtest/` / `tests/factors/` / `tests/performance/` 并列。**所有 AI / 通用 service 测试都进此目录**——下次 US-056 KOLAggregatorService / US-057 MarketSentimentIndexService / US-059 AnnouncementNLPService 测试统一进 `tests/services/`。Node 直接跑 `npx ts-node --transpile-only tests/services/<Name>.test.ts`，不在 tsconfig include 内所以 `npx tsc --noEmit` 不扫描。
- **市场级聚合指数 sigmoid 归一化范式 (US-057 MarketSentimentIndexService 引入)**: 任何 N 维度加权聚合指数 (情绪 / 健康度 / 风险评分) 用 `100 / (1 + exp(-raw/scale))` 自然压扁到 [0,100]，无须 clip/clamp。**scale=30** 让 raw=±30 落在 ~27/73，scale 越大曲线越平。**raw=NaN/Infinity → 50 中性**（早返回防溢出）。**scale ≤ 0 → 50** 防参数错误炸服务。极端值 (|raw/scale| > 50) 提前饱和到 0/100 防 exp 溢出。下次 US-091 因子风险评分 / US-095 系统健康度评分都按此 sigmoid 归一化模式。
- **z-score 横截面算法的 `STDDEV_NEAR_ZERO = 1e-9` 防爆护栏 (US-057 跨 US-035 范式)**: 常数序列 (lookback 全部相同值) stddev=0 → 除零 NaN/Infinity；computeZScore 用 `sd < 1e-9` 阈值返回 null (caller 退化为 0 中性贡献)。同款已在 US-035 FactorICReport / US-038 BootstrapResample 引入。**测试时务必构造非线性时序** (e.g. `[100, 110, 130, 160, 200, ...]` 不等间隔) 否则 diff 序列全相同 z=null 永远过不了非空检查。**`minObservations` 默认 5** (< 此数样本不足返回 null) 与统计学惯例对齐。
- **day-to-day diff 推算"净买入"模式 (US-057 引入)**: 累计型指标 (NorthboundHolding 累计持股 / 融资余额 / 总账户数) 需要 day-to-day diff 才能得到"今日增量"; `computeDailyDiffs(series)` 返回 length-1 数组 (第一日无 diff), 配套 `target = series[last]; history = series.slice(0,-1)` 用。**指标特性 → 是否 diff 的决策树**: 累计量 → 必须 diff (持仓/余额/总数); 流量/事件计数 → 直接用值 (post_count/news_count/订单数)。**Python 层已预算的 net 字段 (US-057 MarginBalance rz_net_buy_yi) 优先用**, 不要 TS 层再 diff (避免双重转换误差)。下次 US-088 持仓变化 / US-092 资金流入累计追踪都按此 diff 决策树。
- **list + latest 端点分工范式 (US-057 引入)**: 存储 heavy 字段 (JSONB / 大 text) 的实体, list endpoint (GET /resource?days=N) 返回**轻量 array 不带 JSONB** (减少时序图 payload, 10x 体积差异); latest endpoint (GET /resource/latest) 返回**单条 + 完整 JSONB** 供详情页 drill-down。下次任何 "时序 + 详情" 类 endpoint (US-058 雪球热词 / US-067 KOL 告警 list / US-073 复盘日记 list) 都按此分工避免 payload 膨胀。
- **"启发式 + 可选 AI" 双路 NLP 范式 (US-059 AnnouncementNLPService 引入)**: 高吞吐文本 NLP (公告 / 问答 / KOL 评论 ~ 几千条/日) 默认走启发式 (关键词字典 + 正则) ≈ 1ms/条免远端调用; UI 主动"详细解读"才设 `extract_with_ai=true` 调远端 30s+。**双路设计 4 要点**: (1) summarize(row, {extract_with_ai}) 单方法两路, caller 不感知 dispatch; (2) AI 失败时 buildNLPResultFromPayload 内 fallback 启发式 + `status='partial'` + `nlp_engine='heuristic_fallback'` 让用户看到部分结果; (3) 启发式字典 (TOPIC_KEYWORDS / ANN_SENTIMENT_KEYWORDS) 全 export 供单测 + 未来按业务调字典只改常量; (4) batch sync 内 for-loop 串行调 summarize (启发式快; AI 路径串行避免远端限流), 不要 Promise.all。下次 US-060 东财问答 NLP / US-061 K 线解读 / US-074 黑天鹅监测同款双路。
- **NLP `status` (completed/partial/failed/pending) × `nlp_engine` (trading_agents/heuristic_fallback/openai) 双列范式 (US-059 引入)**: status 标记**结果状态**, nlp_engine 标记**抽取引擎**, 两列正交。让 ops 同时看"成败率"和"AI 调用占比"两个独立维度: `status='partial' AND nlp_engine='heuristic_fallback'` = AI 失败 fallback, `status='completed' AND nlp_engine='trading_agents'` = AI 成功。**避免合并成单列 ('completed_ai'/'failed_heur'/'partial_ai_fallback')** — 6 个 enum 组合不仅难列举还失去独立筛选能力。下次 US-060 / US-061 / US-062 任何 AI 抽取都按此双列。
- **ISO 周聚合 `computeWeekStart(date)` 范式 (US-060 EastMoneyQATopicService 引入)**: 任何"按周聚合"逻辑 (问答主题统计 / 周报 / 风控周阈值 / 策略周再平衡) 复用 `computeWeekStart` 不要重写。**5 大边界 must-cover**: (a) 周一 self / (b) 周日 → 上周一 (ISO-8601 周日是 day 7 不是 day 0); (c) 跨月 (周日 06-29 → 周一 06-29); (d) 跨年 (周四 01-01 → 周一 12-29); (e) 带 'YYYY-MM-DD HH:mm:ss' 后缀的字符串 (cninfo `question_time` 实际格式). **实现关键**: 用 `getUTCDay() === 0 ? 7 : utcDay` 把 JS 周日的 0 翻成 ISO 的 7, 再减 (isoWeekday - 1) 得周一; 全程 UTC 处理避免本地时区让"周日 23:00" 算到下周一去。**N-tuple PK 用 `(stock_code, week_start, topic)` 复合 UNIQUE**: 同一只股票同周同 topic 只一行, bulkCreate updateOnDuplicate 直接刷新 mention_count + sentiment_score (避免重复 sync 累加污染)。下次 US-061 K 线周技术信号 / US-067 KOL 周观点聚合 / US-088 持仓周变化 都按此 (entity, week_start, dimension) 3-tuple PK。
- **`parseRemoteClassify` AI payload 4 条防御 (US-060 引入)**: 解析远端 AI 返回结构化 (topic / score) 时必 4 条 guard: (1) `status === 'FAILED'` → null (调用方 fallback 启发式); (2) `!data` → null; (3) `sentiment_score` 非 finite (NaN / Infinity) → 0; (4) `sentiment_score` 超 [-1, +1] 范围 → clamp 而非 throw / null。**不要相信远端守 schema** — 任一缺失都让"远端胡说"污染聚合 (e.g. score=5.0 让平均分爆到 1.5 破坏后续阈值判断)。下次 US-061 K 线解读 / US-074 黑天鹅 NLP / US-067 KOL AI 评分都套此 4 条 guard。
- **DataSource fake `Set<key>` 粒度 vs boolean 全开关 (US-060 测试引入)**: 批量 sync 测试 (syncStocks 多只股票) 需要"600519 OK 但 000001 throws"的混合 case — 仅一个 `fetchShouldThrow: boolean` 全 throw / 全 OK 二选一无法覆盖 continue_on_error 真路径。**通用模式**: fake DataSource 状态字段优先 `fetchShouldThrowFor: Set<string>` 粒度而非单 boolean, 让测试能精准模拟 per-entity partial failure。下次任何 batch syncN / persistN / classifyN service 测试都按此粒度设计 fake。
- **24h TTL 缓存范式 (US-061 TechnicalAnalysisService 引入)**: 高成本 + 短时效 (AI K 线解读 / AI 复盘日记 / KOL 观点聚合) 类 service 的存储模式：(1) **不复用 (stock, day) UNIQUE**——同一只股票一天可能多次请求, 应该 hit cache 而非 conflict; (2) **PK = autoIncrement id + 索引 (stock_code, lookback_days, expires_at)** — 旧 cache 行作为历史快照保留 (UI 可回查"上次解读说了什么"), 新 cache 行 INSERT 不 UPDATE; (3) **`expires_at = generated_at + 24h` 列由 service 写入**, 读端 `where expires_at > now() order by generated_at desc limit 1`; (4) **`force_refresh=true` 参数跳过 cache 直接生成** (UI 刷新按钮触发); (5) **缓存查询失败必须 try/catch + warn 不阻塞主流程** (cache miss + 网络问题不应让用户拿不到新结果)。下次 US-067 KOL 观点聚合 / US-073 复盘日记 AI / US-074 黑天鹅监测都按此 5 项 checklist。
- **AI service 复用 QuantMath 而非内置指标 (US-061 引入)**: AI 解读类 service 计算 MACD / RSI / 布林 / 量比 时必须 `import { sma, ema, rsi, macd, bollinger } from '../quant/engine/QuantMath'`, 不要内置或 copy 同算法到 service 层。**理由**: backtest / strategies / AI 解读三处指标口径必须一致, 否则用户在策略实验室看的 RSI=70 和 AI 弹窗里看的 RSI=68 是不同公式 — 信任崩塌。本范式与 US-009 FactorPipeline 复用 QuantMath 一脉相承; 下次 US-074 黑天鹅 / US-091 综合健康度任何用技术指标的地方都从 QuantMath 取。
- **启发式 fallback "强信号 + momentum 兜底" 趋势判定 (US-061 引入)**: 任何"趋势判断"启发式 (uptrend/downtrend/sideways) 不能只看 MACD hist 符号 — MACD 在长期单边行情末段会出现 hist 反弹（DIF/DEA 收敛）但价格仍在下方，单看 MACD 会误判 sideways。**正确逻辑**: (1) 优先 MACD hist + 布林 mid **同向**的强信号 (hist > 0 AND close > mid → uptrend); (2) 强信号缺失时用 N 日 momentum_pct **兜底** (|momentum| > 5% 判方向)。**测试 must-cover 末段 hist 反弹场景** (drift -1% × 60 日的下降趋势，末日 MACD hist 已转正但 momentum 仍 -45%)。下次 US-074 黑天鹅 / US-091 风险评分任何"趋势/方向"判定都套此双层兜底, 不要单维度判定。
- **第 7 个 AI feature — Copilot meta-AI / 自由文本 prompt 范式 (US-062 引入)**: 与前 6 个 AI feature (US-055 单股深度问答 / US-056 KOL / US-057 情绪指数 / US-058 雪球热词 / US-059 公告 NLP / US-060 东财问答 NLP / US-061 K 线解读) 都是 "固定输入 → 结构化输出" 不同，Copilot 是 **"自由文本 prompt → 4 种意图分类 → 分支响应"** 的 meta 形态。**6 项核心模式**: (1) **强→弱 intent 正则优先级**（generate_draft > suggest_params > explain_backtest > general）— 强类必须在弱类之前，否则 "写策略，解释 sharpe" 会被错分到 explain_backtest; 同 US-055 normalizeRecommendation strong_buy > buy 模式; (2) **prompt 模板顺序固定**（System + Strategy + Description + Default params + Recent backtests + User intent + User question）方便后续 prompt tuning 对比; (3) **启发式 fallback 4 种 intent 各自分支文案**（不是一句通用 "AI 不可达请重试" 兜底）; (4) **conversation_id 续接对话上下文**（COP-{YYYYMMDDHHmmss}-{rand4} 业务 ID，前端多轮对话可传同一 ID）; (5) **saveConversation 默认 noop**（每问独立上下文，会话历史价值低；未来引入加密会话表只改 DefaultDataSource）— 与 US-055/061 强制 saveReport 的有结构数据存储相反; (6) **suggested_params + extractKeyDelta 跨 tab 数据流**（Copilot 建议 → diff Table → "应用并去新建回测" 把 params merge 进表单 JSON 不覆盖既有手动改动）。**判据：何时不缓存 (vs US-061 24h TTL)**: 输入维度数少且离散 (stock_code + lookback_days 2 维) → TTL 缓存; 维度多且开放 (prompt 自由文本 + intent + strategy + history) → 不缓存。下次任何"自由文本输入 + 多意图分类 + 跨 tab 上下文" 类 AI feature（US-073 复盘日记 AI 自生成 / 未来"组合优化助手"）按此 6 项模板。
- **Frontend FloatButton + Drawer 聊天模板 (US-062 引入)**: 工作区右下角浮聊天面板的标准布局：`<FloatButton type="primary" icon={<RobotOutlined />} style={{ insetInlineEnd: 32, bottom: 32 }} />` 触发 `<Drawer placement="right" width={480} />`。**关键 styling**: `styles={{ body: { display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', padding: 0 } }}` 让内部 4 块（顶部 context / quick-prompts / 消息列表 flex 1 / 输入框）撑满。**ChatBubble 子组件**: 用户右对齐 + 蓝底白字 + UserOutlined; 助手左对齐 + 白底 + RobotOutlined; pending 显 `<Spin />` + "正在思考..."。**消息列表 background = #f6f8fa** chat 体感一致 (类 Slack/微信)。**跨 tab 应用模式**: onApply 回调 = merge-not-overwrite (`{...existingJSON, ...suggestedParams}`) + setActiveKey 切到目标 tab + message.success 反馈。下次 US-074 黑天鹅"AI 风控提示弹层" / US-091 系统健康度浮窗 / 任何"AI 助手 panel"都复用此 5 件套模板（FloatButton + Drawer + ChatBubble + merge-not-overwrite + tab 切换）。

### Push notifications — outbound channels (US-063+)
- **第 1 个推送类 feature 模板 (US-063 DailyTradingDigestService 引入)**: 后续 US-064 (飞书业绩预告即时提醒) / US-065 (邮件周报 SMTP) / US-066 (微信公众号订阅消息) / US-067 (高优先级 RiskAlert 三通道并行) 全部按此 8 项 checklist：(1) **`User.risk_config.notification_channels` JSONB namespace 共享**（与 position_limits / trailing_stop / drawdown_breaker 并列，免去新表，复用 US-017 `.changed('risk_config', true)` JSONB mutation 模式）。`feishu` / `email` / `wechat` 三个子对象。(2) **`normalizeNotificationConfig(raw)` 静默退回默认**（与 US-047..US-055 normalizeXxxConfig 一致）— 用户改坏一项不让整 service 4xx。(3) **`shouldSendForUser(config, hasEnvFallback)` 多路径 gate** 至少含 "通道未启用 / 该类消息关闭 / 缺少接收地址 + env fallback" 3 路径。(4) **DataSource 注入式 send**（`sendXxxCard(payload, url)` / `sendEmail(payload, addr)` / `sendWeChatMessage(payload, openid)`）— 实际 webhook/SMTP/微信调用走 DataSource 接口，单测 fake 完全脱外部依赖。(5) **每用户独立 try/catch**: per-user 串行 await + outer try/catch wrap，单用户失败不阻塞批量。(6) **`dry_run=true` 选项**: 让 UI 预览 payload 不实际推送；前端配合 "预览 + Modal.confirm 二次确认推送" 双层 UX 保护。(7) **业务 ID `{TYPE}-{user_id}-{YYYYMMDD}-{rand4}`** 与 US-055 命名范式一致 (`DIGEST-` / `EARN-` / `WEEKLY-` / `WECHAT-` / `ALERT-` 前缀)。(8) **scheduler cron + manual trigger 双入口**: cron 走 SchedulerService 一个 task type (`PAPER_TRADING_DAILY_DIGEST` / `EARNINGS_FORECAST_PUSH` / `WEEKLY_REVIEW_EMAIL` 等)；manual 走 SettingsController POST endpoint；**共用同一 service.sendXxx() 方法**保证 cron 跑出与用户手动跑出的结果完全一致。完整参考实现：`backend/src/services/DailyTradingDigestService.ts`。
- **`sendXxxCard(payload, url, {buildCard})` 注入式 buildCard 范式 (US-063 引入)**: FeishuBotWebhookService / EmailNotificationService / WeChatOAService 共享底层 channel adapter，但每种 card/邮件/消息模板的 JSON 结构由 caller (DailyTradingDigestService / EarningsForecastWatcher / WeeklyReviewReportService) 决定。**正确做法**: caller 把 `buildCard(payload): cardJSON` 函数传进 adapter，adapter 只负责"调 buildCard + 调远端 + 解析 code/msg + fail-OPEN 返回"，避免反向依赖。否则 adapter 必须 import 所有 caller 形成 N×M cycle。下次 US-064/065/066/067 加新 card/email/message 类型，FeishuBotWebhookService / EmailNotificationService 不用改一行。
- **Modal.confirm 二次确认对真实外部发送 (US-063 引入)**: 任何"按钮点了 → 实际外部副作用（发邮件 / 推送飞书 / 调起短信 / 调用第三方 API）" 都必须 `Modal.confirm({title, content, okButtonProps:{type:'primary'}, onOk: async()=>{...}})` 二次确认，纯前端 / dry-run / 本地 state 修改不需要。下次 US-064/065/066/067 都用此规则。
- **`per_user` 长度 = 1 当 user_id filter (US-063 引入)**: `sendXxxDigests({user_id})` 让 service 过滤到单用户，per_user 数组长度恰好 1（或 0 如果该 user 不存在）。前端 single-user 触发 endpoint 直接 `result.per_user[0]` 取 single result；admin 多用户批量 endpoint 不传 user_id 拿全数组。**service 层不区分单/多 user 模式**，只看是否传 user_id；controller 决定单/多语义。下次任何 single-user 触发的批量类 endpoint (US-064 EarningsForecastWatcher / US-067 RealtimeAlertDispatcher) 按此 controller 收敛单 user 模式。
- **`react/no-unescaped-entities` 中文 JSX 双引号必须 `&quot;` (US-063 lint 教训)**: `"立即推送"` 在 JSX 文本节点会被 ESLint react/no-unescaped-entities 拦截。**解决**: 用 `&quot;` HTML entity 或换全角引号 `「」`/`『』`。**判据**: 任何 JSX 文本含 `"` / `'` 立即转义，不要等 lint 报错重跑（prettier 还会自作主张转换单/双引号造成死循环）。
- **2 cron path 模式 — held 即时 + watchlist 盘后聚合 (US-064 引入)**: 同类数据 (业绩预告 / 风控告警 / 异动事件) 因用户决策延迟容忍度不同走两条 path。**判据**: (a) 持仓相关 → 必须当日内反应 (e.g. 持仓股财报超预期能涨停) → 即时 cron `*/15 9-15 * * 1-5` + 每条独立 card; (b) 自选/未持仓 → 盘后聚合可接受 → 盘后 cron `35 15 * * 1-5` + 一条 digest card 汇总。**实现**: 同 service 两个 method (`scanHeldStocks` / `scanWatchlistStocks`) 共享 DataSource + 私有 helpers；单 scheduler task type 带 `mode='held'|'watchlist'|'both'` parameter 多态化。**核心约束**: `pickForecastsForWatchers(forecasts, watchedSet, heldSet)` 必须传 heldSet 参数, filter 排除持仓股避免双推 (held path 已即时发了一条独立 card)。下次 US-067 HIGH 告警即时 + MEDIUM 聚合 / US-091 异动事件持仓即时 + 全市场盘后 复用此双 cron 模式。
- **dedup buffer FIFO LRU 200 条 — JSONB 持久化 (US-053 BlackSwanWatchdog + US-064 EarningsForecastWatcher 第 2 处)**: 高频 cron (每 15 min) 需要持久化 dedup 防重复推送，不能用 in-memory Set (process restart 丢失)。**正确做法**: `User.risk_config.<namespace>_seen` JSONB array 存 signature 字符串，FIFO LRU bump 重复 signature 到末尾，超过 200 条 drop 老的。`mergeSeenSignatures(existing, newOnes, limit)` 是 pure function 让单测覆盖 LRU 边界 / 重复 signature 不增长 / limit 边界。**Signature 设计原则**: 业务唯一性维度全部进 signature (e.g. `${path}::ANN::${stock_code}::${announce_date}::${report_period}`)，修订公告 = 不同 announce_date = 新 signature = 允许再发 (业务正确)。下次 US-067 风控告警 (`{level}::ALERT::${rule_id}::${alert_date}`) / US-091 健康度评分 (`HEALTH::${component}::${check_date}`) 都按此模板。
- **DataSource method 拆分原则 (US-064 引入)**: 一个 DB query / 一个外部调用 = 一个 DataSource method, 不要 inline 多个 query 在一个 method (单测 fake 难表达分支)。US-064 EarningsForecastWatcher 拆 8 个 method (`listEligibleUsers / loadRecentForecasts / loadHeldStockCodes / loadWatchedStockCodes / loadAnalystConsensus / loadStockMeta / saveSeenSignatures / sendFeishuCard`) 比 US-063 DailyTradingDigest 5 个 method 多 3 个，原因: held + watchlist 2 path 需 2 loader, 加 consensus & dedup persist 各 1。**复用判据**: 跨 service 共享的 method (`listEligibleUsers`) 看 query 形状是否完全相同 — 概念相似但 boolean 子开关不同就各自实现，简化 fake；不要为"复用" 抽 abstract base class。下次 US-067 DataSource 按需扩展。
- **跨年度 EPS 严格过滤 — 一致预期推送层 (US-064 引入, 复用 US-030/032 因子层模式)**: AnalystForecast.forecast_eps_y1 字段含义随年份滚动 (2024 末 Y1=2025 → 2025 末 Y1=2026)。同一只股取近 90 日所有研报均值时**必须**按 `forecast_year_y1 = year(report_period)` 严格过滤目标年度，否则跨年度 silently 错位。同款约束 US-030/032 已在因子层确立，US-064 扩展到推送层。**实现**: `loadAnalystConsensus(stock, asOfDate, lookbackDays)` 先 forecast_year_y1 过滤再 mean()，少于 `MIN_REPORTS=2` 返回 null，UI 显示 "—" 不阻塞 push。下次任何"跨年度财务指标聚合" (US-091 估值合理性评分 / US-100 ETF 业绩对比) 都按此严格过滤。
- **区间数值 4 路径格式化 (US-064 `formatProfitChangeRange` 引入)**: AKShare 公司业绩预告等区间字段 (low, high) 不保证都有值 — 公司可能只公告下限或上限。**4 路径兜底**: 都有 → '+50.0% ~ +80.0%'; 仅 low → '≥ +50.0%'; 仅 high → '≤ +80.0%'; 都没 → '—'。**关键 guard**: `Number.isFinite(Number(v))` 同时过滤 null / undefined / NaN / Infinity / 空字符串, 不要单一 `!== null` 判定 (会把 NaN 当合法值 fmt 成 "NaN%")。同款 4 路径模式适用其他业绩区间字段 (营收增速 / 毛利率波动 / 股息率范围 / 季度 ROE 区间)。
- **scanXxx public method 共享 _ 私有 helper (US-064 引入)**: service 内 `_pushEvent(payload, dryRun)` / `_persistSeen(user, signature)` / `_loadConsensusForRow(row, asOfDate)` 让多个 public scanXxx method 共享 webhook send + dedup persist + consensus load 逻辑。**判据**: 同 service 内 2+ public method 共享 3+ 步骤 → 抽 _ 前缀 private method (TS 不强制 # private, 是为单测可达); 单 method 内 1-2 步骤 → 内联不抽。下次任何多入口 service (US-067 4 method 不同 alert 级别 / US-091 多维度健康度 scanXxx) 按此模式抽 helper。
- **OAuth-style 绑定流程范式 — 微信公众号 (US-066 引入)**: 与 US-063 飞书 / US-065 邮件并列的第 3 个推送 channel，但新增 OAuth-style 绑定流程（用户必须扫码 → 关注公众号 → 后端拿到 openid 才能发）。**6 项模板** (适用未来任何 OAuth-style channel: 钉钉 / Slack / Telegram): (1) **scene_str 编码 user_id + rand6 双层** `bind-{user_id}-{rand6}` ≤ 64 字符 ASCII 限制，parseBindSceneStr 直接反解 user_id + rand6 防"两个用户先后扫码"混淆 + 防猜测；(2) **scene_str 持久化到 user.risk_config.notification_channels.wechat.bind_scene_str** 让 webhook SCAN 事件 belt-and-suspenders 反查 user (一次靠 parseBindSceneStr / 一次靠 DB 行)，同一 user 多次扫码 latest 覆盖让旧二维码失效；(3) **前端轮询 confirmBind endpoint** 每 3s 查 openid 是否填好，**40 次 (2min) 硬上限**防僵尸轮询；超过设 `status='expired'` UI 显示 warning + 重新生成按钮；(4) **access_token 缓存** 2h TTL + 提前 5min 主动刷新 + module-level let cachedToken (禁用 class field 避免多 instance 缓存失效) + 提供 resetWeChatAccessToken() 公开 helper 让单测/ops 手动 invalidate；(5) **失效 (errcode=40001/40014) 单次重试** 不递归避 stack 爆 + invalidate 缓存 + 重拉；(6) **sendByTemplate 主流程** 取 templateId(env) → loadUserConfig → shouldSend gate → buildPayload (closure 捕获 options) try/catch → dry_run 短路 → 调远端 → 包 SendXxxResult。下次 US-067 阿里云短信 / 未来钉钉 / Slack 任何 OAuth-style channel 按此 6 项 checklist。
- **simulate/replay/impersonate dev endpoint production-mode 404 + env opt-in 双重 gate (US-066 引入)**: 任何允许 caller "代表他人写状态" / "模拟外部事件" 的 dev endpoint (webhook simulator / hook 触发器 / cron 手动跑)必须 production-mode 404 + 显式 env opt-in (e.g. `ALLOW_WECHAT_BIND_SIMULATE=true`) 双重 gate。**判据**: endpoint 接受 caller 提供的 user_id / scene_str / signature 这类 "本来应该由可信第三方提供" 的输入 → 必走此 gate。否则任何登录用户拿到他人的 scene_str (前端泄露 / log 泄露 / 截屏泄露) 偷绑账号。范例: `if (process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production' || String(process.env.ALLOW_X || '').toLowerCase() !== 'true') return res.status(404).json({...})`。
- **前端 useRef + window.setInterval 轮询 cleanup 5 项 checklist (US-066 引入)**: modal-driven polling 走此 5 项: (1) `pollRef = useRef<number | null>(null)` 持有 interval id; (2) `window.setInterval(...)` 返回值赋给 ref; (3) `stopPolling()` 内部判 non-null 才 `clearInterval`; (4) `useEffect(() => () => stopPolling(), [stopPolling])` 组件 unmount 自动 cleanup; (5) 轮询次数硬上限 (e.g. 40 次 = 2min) 防"用户开了模态就走 / 忘了" 永远 polling 直到关 tab。下次 US-067 风控告警实时状态轮询 / US-091 监控面板 polling 按此 checklist 实现。
- **共享 JSONB schema 加新字段不破坏 sibling test 范式 (US-066 验证)**: 给 NotificationChannelsConfig / 任何共享 JSONB schema 加字段时, normalizeXxxConfig 必须用 `safeBoolean(raw, default)` + `safeString(raw)` **不写 undefined** —— 旧 risk_config JSONB 行不含新字段时, normalize 自动填默认值, sibling tests (US-066 给 wechat 加 4 字段时 / DailyTradingDigest 174 + EarningsForecastWatcher 184 + WeeklyReviewReport 225 = 583 个测试) 不需要改 fixture。**判据**: "新字段 = optional + safe default"，不要硬要求 caller 显式填。NotificationChannelsConfig.wechat 在 US-066 加 4 字段 (bind_scene_str / bound_at / earnings_alert / risk_alert) 是参考实现。
- **第 4 channel SMS 加入 + alert hook 范式 (US-067 RealtimeAlertDispatcher 引入)**: 与 US-063/064/065/066 的 cron 触发模式互补——本 channel 触发源是 **`@AfterCreate` model hook** (RiskAlert level=HIGH 即触发 dispatcher.fireAndForget())。**5 项新模式**: (1) **dedup signature = `${rule_id}::${symbol}::${level}`** 不含 alert.id（含 id 让每个 alert 唯一 = dedup 失效，business signature 是"同一类告警"的语义维度集合）。(2) **JSONB + timestamp 比较代替 Redis TTL**: 本项目暂未引入 Redis，AC "Redis 去重" 实质需求是"持久化 + TTL 窗"——用 `user.risk_config.realtime_alert_seen` JSONB array + FIFO LRU 200 条 + `isWithinDedupWindow(pushed_at_ms, now_ms)` pure function 比较代替 Redis EXPIRE。优劣权衡: 不依赖 Redis 基础设施 / 单测可控 / 用户级 isolated；劣势 = 高并发 race condition（缓解：alertCreate hook 串行 + dedup 是降噪不是 idempotency）。下次 US-094 异动事件 / US-097 异常监控等去重需求按此 pattern。(3) **4 status 精确语义** (sent / partial / failed / skipped): sent = 至少 1 channel 成功 + 全部成功；partial = 至少 1 成功 + 至少 1 失败；failed = 0 成功 + ≥1 失败；skipped = 0 channel 真派（顶层 level gate / 用户缺失 / dedup 命中 / 所有 channel 各自 skip 或全 disabled）。前端 Alert 颜色 success/warning/error/info 一一对齐。下次 US-077 风控告警中心 / US-091 健康度推送同款 4 类不漏（partial 最易漏）。(4) **Sequelize `@AfterCreate static methodName(instance)` 3 大坑**: hook throw 让 .create() 失败 (顶层 try/catch 吞错) / 不能 await hook 内 fire-and-forget 调用 / lazy-require 反向依赖避循环 import。(5) **lazy-require 第三方 SDK (阿里云 / 腾讯云 / openai)** 让 SDK 缺失时 import + typecheck 仍 OK，第一次 send 才 require + fail-OPEN catch + warn。下次任何"按需远端 SDK" connector 走此 5 项 checklist。完整 9 项设计 in `backend/src/portfolio/CLAUDE.md` `risk/RealtimeAlertDispatcher hook (US-067)` 一节。
- **`RiskAlert.rule_id` 字段所有 writer 必填清单 (US-067 补丁固化)**：rule_id 是 nullable column 但实质 dispatcher dedup signature 必需—— 不填让所有 HIGH 告警退化成 `unknown::symbol::HIGH` 同 signature 在 30 min 窗口内互相 dedup 拦掉。**8 个写入点 + 命名映射** (snake_case, 与 guard 文件名简写一致): `position_limit` (PositionLimitGuard) / `trailing_stop` (TrailingStopGuard) / `per_stock_stop_loss` (PerStockStopLossGuard) / `drawdown_breaker` (DrawdownCircuitBreaker) / `market_regime_alert` (MarketRegimeAlertService) / `black_swan` (BlackSwanWatchdog) / `industry_concentration` (IndustryConcentrationGuard) / `factor_correlation` (FactorCorrelationReport)。**同 guard 内多 sub-level (DrawdownCircuitBreaker LEVEL_1/2/3) 共享一个 rule_id**——不要为每个 level 拆 rule_id 否则 30 min 内 3 条卡片同推（UX 灾难）；LEVEL 升级时新 signature 写入会覆盖旧 dedup 记录是合理行为。**新 guard 写 RiskAlert.create() 必填 rule_id**——加 guard 的同时给 portfolio/CLAUDE.md 表格新增一行。

### Ops — env validation / startup gates (US-068+)
- **`backend/src/config/EnvValidator.ts` 是单一 env 校验源 (US-068 引入)**：所有"需要在启动前确认 env 完整"的 caller 都走 `validateEnv(env?, {customSchema?})` → `EnvValidationResult { ok, errors[], warnings[], validated, node_env }`。**不复用 zod / envalid**——项目已有 `joi` 依赖（`StrategyEngine` / 数据 schema 验证已在用），Joi 的 `.required()` + `.default()` + `.uri()` + `.valid()` + `unknown(true)` 已覆盖 4 大类字段（必填 / 可选有默认 / 可选无默认 / 未知 env 允许）的全部需求。下次任何"启动前校验"需求（live-trading env subset / scheduler-only env subset）按 customSchema 参数复用本模块，不要平行实现。
- **`shouldExitOnFailure(result)` 判 production 模式才 exit (US-068 引入)**：production 任何 error 立即 exit(1) — 半夜 missing config 不能让 backend 启起来留 zombie；development / test 模式 errors > 0 不 exit 让单测 / 本地探索可以不准备完整 env 跑（fixture 模式）。**index.ts 第 1-25 行**: dotenv 后立即跑 validateEnv + log errors/warnings + 判 shouldExitOnFailure → process.exit(1)。**关键 invariant**：env 校验必须**在所有其他 import 之前**——下游 service / config / queue (queue.ts / database.ts) 在 import-time 就读 process.env；validate 放在 import 链中间已经太晚。
- **4 channel group 部分填写 = error (US-068 引入)**：Feishu / SMTP / WeChat / Aliyun SMS 任一字段填了就视为"用户表达了启用此通道的意图"，缺其他字段必须升级 error（不当 warning）—— 用户已经投入意图（填了 SMTP_HOST），缺 SMTP_PASS 静默继续会让推送 silent 失败。`detectPartialChannelGroup(env, group)` 是纯函数复用 — 下次任何"打包配置组"（OAuth client_id+secret / API key+endpoint 对）按此模式校验。**全空 → production 仅 warning 不阻塞**（用户未表达启用意图），**全填齐 → 通过**。
- **占位符值检测 (US-068 引入)**：`PLACEHOLDER_VALUES` 常量列出 8 个已知占位符 (`your_jwt_secret_key_here` / `change-me` / `TODO` ...)。production 模式遇占位符 → error (production_required)；development 模式 → warning (placeholder_value)。**新加占位符必须 append 到 PLACEHOLDER_VALUES** —— 同步 .env.example 与 EnvValidator 的占位符字典，避免运维更新模板但忘了同步校验列表。
- **`npm run check-env` 是 CLI 独立入口 (US-068 引入)**：`backend/src/scripts/check-env.ts` 走同一份 validateEnv，stdout 输出 errors / warnings 报告 + exit code 0/1。CI 流水线 / 部署 preflight / 开发者本地 debug 都用同一个命令 — "启动失败"和"check-env 失败"输出**完全相同的 errors 列表**。新加 env 字段后必须更新 .env.example 注释 + EnvValidator schema 同步（CI 应该跑 check-env 检测漂移）。

### Ops — GitHub Actions CI (US-069+)
- **`.github/workflows/ci.yml` 是项目唯一的 typecheck/lint/test CI workflow (US-069 引入)**：与 `security-lint.yml` 互补但完全独立 — 后者只跑弱密钥 / 审计事件 grep 检查，本 workflow 跑 backend (typecheck + lint + test) + frontend (typecheck + lint)。**未来加任何"运行时质量门"** (US-091 健康度 / US-100 集成 smoke test) 走第 3 个独立 workflow 文件，不堆到 ci.yml — 单文件超过 300 行变 unmaintainable。
- **paths-filter@v3 + `|| github.event_name == 'pull_request'` 双条件**：本地分支 push 仅相关目录变化跑（省 CI 时间）；PR 必跑全（防漏检跨目录隐含影响）。**`needs.detect-changes.outputs.X == 'true' || github.event_name == 'pull_request'`** 是新 workflow job condition 范本，下次任何分组 CI（独立 lint / 独立测试套）按同款。
- **actions/setup-node@v4 内置 cache 选项替代 actions/cache@v4**：`cache: 'npm'` + `cache-dependency-path: <pkg>/package-lock.json` 两行 = 自动按 lock-hash key 缓存 ~/.npm。比旧版手写 `actions/cache@v4 with key: ${{ runner.os }}-node-${{ hashFiles(...) }}` 少 6 行 YAML 且 GitHub 官方维护。新 CI step 加 Node 一律走内置 cache。
- **失败时 actions/github-script@v7 createComment + tee 出 .log**：每 step `<cmd> 2>&1 | tee ../<step>.log`，failure() 触发的 script 用 GITHUB_WORKSPACE 拼路径 + `.split('\n').slice(-60)` 截尾 + `<details><summary>` 折叠片段。下次任何"CI 失败信息推 PR"按同款 — 比 actions/upload-artifact 单独看更直接。
- **concurrency.cancel-in-progress: true 必加**：同分支重复 push 自动取消旧 run，节省 60%+ runner 时间。生产 repo 必加；研究 / 数据 repo 才用 false 保留 run 历史。

### Ops — backup / restore shell scripts (US-071+)
- **运维脚本 = 仓库根 `scripts/<task>-<resource>.sh` (US-071 引入)**：所有"管理员手动 / cron 触发"的运维脚本放在仓库根 `scripts/` 目录（与 `scripts/deployment/` / `scripts/setup_and_db/` 既有子目录并列），命名形如 `<verb>-<resource>.sh` (`backup-db.sh` / `restore-db.sh` / 未来 `rotate-logs.sh` / `purge-cache.sh`)。不要放进 `backend/src/scripts/`——后者是 `ts-node` 跑的开发期 CLI（同步数据 / 计算因子 / 跑回测）需要 backend 的 TS 上下文，运维脚本是纯 shell 不该绑 ts-node。**npm script 入口在 `backend/package.json`** 走 `bash ../scripts/<x>.sh`（相对路径 from `backend/`），让 `npm run db:backup` / `npm run db:restore -- --date=...` 与同目录 `db:reset` / `db:migrate` 体感一致。**用户可以直接 `bash scripts/<x>.sh`（仓库根）或 `npm run`（backend/）** 二选一，文档同时给两种用法。
- **shell 脚本通用 8 项 checklist (US-071 引入)**: 任何运维 shell 脚本走此 8 项: (1) **`#!/usr/bin/env bash` + `set -euo pipefail`** 强制 fail-fast (任一 cmd 失败 / 未定义变量 / pipe 中段失败立即退出); (2) **顶部 docstring 包含**: 功能说明 / 环境变量 / 选项 / 使用范例 / cron 范例; (3) **依赖工具用 `command -v <tool>` 校验 + 友好 ERROR 退出** (不要让脚本跑到一半因 pg_dump not found 给出 cryptic exit 127); (4) **绝对路径解析**: `SCRIPT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"` + `REPO_ROOT="$(cd "$SCRIPT_DIR/.." && pwd)"` 让 cron 在任意 cwd 下都能找路径; (5) **可覆盖环境变量配置**: `${VAR:-default}` 范式，每个 env var 在 docstring 列默认值 + 含义; (6) **`for arg in "$@"; case "$arg" in --flag=*) VAR="${arg#--flag=}";;` 解析参数** + 强制 `print_usage` + `-h|--help` 路径; (7) **危险操作 (delete / overwrite / drop) 必须二次确认** + `--yes` 跳过确认让自动化能跑; (8) **临时文件 trap cleanup + 原子 rename**: `TMP="$OUT.tmp.$$"; trap 'rm -f "$TMP"' EXIT; do_work > "$TMP"; mv "$TMP" "$OUT"; trap - EXIT` —— 避免备份中途被 cron / 读端见到半成品。**`backup-db.sh` 与 `restore-db.sh` 是参考实现**，下次任何 ops 脚本 (US-076+ 日志轮转 / 缓存清理 / 数据归档) 按此 8 项照搬。
- **PostgreSQL `pg_dump` / `psql` 通过 `PGPASSWORD` env 传密码 (US-071 引入)**：不要走 `-W` 交互式输入 (cron 不可用) 不要走 `~/.pgpass` (跨服务器迁移要 sync 多个文件)。`PGPASSWORD="$DB_PASSWORD" pg_dump ...` 是 cron-friendly 唯一安全做法。**`pg_dump` 必带 `--no-owner --no-privileges`** 让 restore 跨机器 (生产 DB 用户名是 `prod_app`, 恢复到 dev 用户名是 `postgres`) 不会因 role 不存在报错。**`psql restore` 必带 `-v ON_ERROR_STOP=1`** 否则中间一行 SQL 失败后续 SQL 继续跑产生半 restore 库 (静默灾难)。
- **`find -mtime +N` cleanup 必须 anchor 文件名 pattern 避免误删 (US-071 引入)**：`find "$BACKUP_DIR" -maxdepth 1 -type f -name '[0-9][0-9][0-9][0-9]-[0-9][0-9]-[0-9][0-9].sql.gz' -mtime "+${RETENTION_DAYS}"` —— anchor `YYYY-MM-DD.sql.gz` glob 让脚本只动**自己创建的**备份文件，不动用户手动放的 `manual-snapshot.sql.gz` / `pre-upgrade.tar.gz`。**单纯 `find -mtime +30 -delete`** 是运维灾难 (会顺手删 README / 配置文件 / 用户副本)。RETENTION_DAYS=0 关闭清理用 `if [ "$RETENTION_DAYS" -gt 0 ]` 包住。**`while IFS= read -r -d '' f; do ...; done < <(find ... -print0)`** 是文件名含空格也安全的 find 迭代范式 (`-print0` + `-d ''` null-delimited)。
- **二次确认 + `--yes` 双路径 (US-071 引入)**: 默认要 `read -r CONFIRM` + `if [ "$CONFIRM" != "YES" ] then exit`; CI / cron / 自动化场景加 `--yes` 跳过。**判据**: 操作"可能丢失或覆盖**用户数据**"必走此模板 (restore-db / drop-table / purge-cache); 操作只生成新文件不动既有数据 (backup-db) 不需要。**确认词用 `YES` 全大写** 而非 `y/yes`——降低误按回车造成的 false-confirm。
- **`backups/` + `*.sql.gz` 加 .gitignore (US-071 引入)**: 备份产物绝对不能进 git (含敏感生产数据 + 文件体量大)。**判据**: 任何 "脚本输出 + 永远不该 commit" 的目录或扩展名一并加 .gitignore (logs/ / coverage/ / dist/ 已经在; backups/ + *.sql.gz 是 US-071 新增)。下次 US-072 Prometheus exporter / US-075 集成测试产物等"运行时产物" 同款。
- **README §运维章节模板 (US-071 引入)**: 任何"运维脚本对外文档"走此 6-section 模板: 立即触发 (示例命令 + 产物路径) / 环境变量表 (表格列出每个 var + 默认 + 用途) / 完整参数 (--flag=value 含义 + 互斥关系) / Cron 配置 (具体 `m h * * * cmd` 行 + PATH= 故障排查) / 故障操作指南 (5 步式恢复 SOP) / 注意事项 (安全 / 权限 / 依赖工具安装)。**判据**: 任何"个数 ≥ 2 的 ops 脚本族"必须挂 README 一节，否则继任运维 6 个月后必踩坑。下次 US-072 / US-075 / US-079 任何 ops 流程同款。

### Ops — observability / Prometheus metrics (US-072+)
- **指标层架构 — 单例 `backend/src/metrics/PrometheusRegistry.ts` (US-072 引入)**: 整个进程一份 `prom-client.Registry`，所有 metric 注册在 `createPrometheusRegistry()` 工厂内一次性完成（避免多模块 new Counter 撞名 `Error: A metric with the name X has already been registered`）。`getPrometheusBundle()` 是 lazy singleton，**业务代码绝不直接 import prom-client**——只 import 一个语义化 helper (`incrementOrderTotal` / `observeAIRequestDuration` / `incrementBacktestTotal`) 在内部 try/catch 兜底。新 metric 加 5 步: (1) 在 `createPrometheusRegistry()` 注册一个新 `Counter` / `Gauge` / `Histogram`; (2) 给 `PrometheusMetricsBundle` 加字段; (3) 加 label 类型别名 (`export type XxxLabel = 'a' | 'b'`); (4) 加业务 helper 包封 `bundle.xxx.inc(...)`; (5) 加单测构造 fresh registry 验证 / 更新 `docs/monitoring.md`。下次 US-094 异动事件计数 / US-097 异常监控 / 任何"新 metric" 按此 5 步。
- **`/metrics` endpoint 故意不加鉴权 (US-072)**: Prometheus scraper 在内网 + 通过 reverse proxy / firewall 控访问，**任何 auth middleware 都会让 scraping 失败**——抓取端 cardinality 暴跌 → Grafana 全部空。真访问控制由网络层做不由应用做。Content-Type 必须用 `bundle.registry.contentType`（prom-client 规定的 `text/plain; version=0.0.4; charset=utf-8`），不要硬编码。500 路径返回 `# metrics collection error: ...` 注释行（Prometheus 看到 `#` 开头自动跳过保持 scraper 健康）。
- **label cardinality 红线 — `route` 必须用 Express 模板 (US-072 引入)**: 这是 Prometheus 第一杀手。`resolveRouteLabel(req)` 优先级 `req.route.path + req.baseUrl` (`/api/portfolio/:id` 模板) > `req.baseUrl` (mount path fallback) > `'unmatched'` (兜底 404)。**绝不**用 `req.originalUrl` / `req.url` / `req.path`——含 user_id / stock_code / query string 每个唯一 URL 一个时间序列直接让 Prometheus OOM。同款规则适用 `code` label 用 enum 不用 message 字符串 (`inferOrderFailureCode()` 把 legacy throw 的中文 message 归一化成稳定码 `INSUFFICIENT_FUNDS` / `INVALID_PARAMS`)。**新增 throw 优先 `err.code = 'XXX'`** 直接绕过 message 归一化；只在 fallback 路径才靠 message 模板匹配。下次任何新 label 设计先想"取值集合上限"，超 100 直接拒。
- **三大业务事件埋点位置定 (US-072)**: (1) **`order_total{direction,status,code}`** 在 `PaperTradingFacade.placeOrder` 外层 try/catch wrapper（`_placeOrderInner` 是私有实现）—— success 路径走 `code='ok'`，failure 路径走 `err.code` 或 `inferOrderFailureCode(err.message)`；(2) **`backtest_total{strategy,result}`** 在 `QuantBacktestService.processBacktestTask` 成功路径按 `resultsWithValidation` 每条 +1（一个 task 多策略 per-strategy 计数），`markTaskFailed` 失败路径用 `task.parameters.strategy_keys` 反推；(3) **`ai_request_duration_seconds{provider,endpoint,status}`** 通过 `timedAIRequest(endpoint, () => axios.post(...))` wrapper 包裹 `AIAdvisorService` 所有远程调用，**失败也 observe duration** 让 Grafana 看到 timeout 类故障的耗时分布。新业务事件按此模板：找最薄的 service-layer 入口点 wrap + try/catch 兜底 + label 用 enum 类型化。
- **测试 fresh registry 不动 singleton (US-072 引入)**: `backend/tests/metrics/<name>.test.ts` 走与 strategies / factors / risk / services / backtest tests 一致的 node-direct 约定（`npx ts-node --transpile-only tests/metrics/<x>.test.ts` / 不依赖 jest / async function test... + main()）。**关键约束**: 测试**绝不** import singleton bundle，每次 `createPrometheusRegistry()` factory 单独构造 fresh registry，避免测试间互相污染（一次 `inc()` 会污染所有后续 assert）。覆盖维度: 纯函数 (normalizeMethodLabel / normalizeStatusLabel / resolveRouteLabel) / metric 注册存在性 / business helper 调用 + label 值 / NaN/Infinity / unknown fallback / express middleware (用 mock req/res) / Prometheus text 格式输出 (`# HELP` + `# TYPE` + metric 行 + Histogram `_bucket` `_count` `_sum`) / content type。下次任何新 metric 测试照搬此 8 维度模板。
- **`docs/monitoring.md` 是单一事实源 (US-072 引入)**: 加 metric 必须**同步更新** `docs/monitoring.md` 加 (a) metric 说明表 (label / 含义); (b) 常见 PromQL 查询; (c) alerting rule 建议 (Prometheus rules.yml 格式); (d) 故障排查表行 (例如 "时间序列爆炸 → 检查是否有人用了 req.originalUrl")。**判据**: 一个新 metric 不写到 `monitoring.md` 等于不存在——三个月后没人记得这 metric 怎么查，Grafana 面板做不了，alerting 加不了。docs 与代码必须同期 commit，不能 "后面补"。

---



### Tooling
- **Frontend node_modules are in the main repo, not the worktree.** Run `ln -s /Users/bytedance/go/src/github.com/bruinxz/stocks/frontend/node_modules frontend/node_modules` inside the worktree before `./node_modules/.bin/tsc --noEmit` or eslint. REMOVE the symlink before `git add` — gitignore matches the directory pattern but not the symlink file.
- **Frontend lint command:** `cd frontend && ./node_modules/.bin/eslint src --ext .ts,.tsx --quiet` (errors only). Warnings are prettier nags — use `--fix` to auto-resolve.
- **Frontend typecheck command:** `cd frontend && ./node_modules/.bin/tsc --noEmit` (exits 0 = clean).
- **Backend lint command:** `cd backend && ./node_modules/.bin/eslint src --ext .ts --quiet`. NOTE: the repo currently has ~500+ pre-existing prettier formatting errors in unrelated files — running `--fix` repo-wide would create churn. When changing a file, only fix lint errors that touch your diff.
- **Backend typecheck command:** `cd backend && npx tsc --noEmit` (exits 0 = clean).
- **Browser verification:** No `dev-browser` MCP skill is configured in this environment. UI stories should fall back to manual verification and note that in progress.txt.
- **Factor unit tests live in `backend/tests/factors/`** (US-029, mirroring `backend/tests/strategies/` and `backend/tests/backtest/`). Run via `cd backend && npx ts-node --transpile-only tests/factors/<Name>Factor.test.ts`. The dir is outside tsconfig `include` so `tsc --noEmit` doesn't scan it; tests stay DB-free (assert pure functions + Map metadata + empty-universe path).

## 2026/06/06 - US-001

### What was implemented
Consolidated the frontend from a 38-page menu to **6 unified workspaces**:

- `/workspace/today` (今日作战)
- `/workspace/factors` (选股因子)
- `/workspace/lab` (策略实验室)
- `/workspace/portfolio` (持仓与复盘)
- `/workspace/data` (数据中心)
- `/workspace/settings` (账号设置)

All legacy pages listed in the PRD acceptance criteria (StrategyExperimentLab, AutonomousOptimizationLab, QuantBacktestLab, QuantPerformanceDashboard, QuantSignalPool, QuantStrategyLibrary, Strategy, Recommendations, RecommendationPerformance, RecommendationTradeOutcomes, RecommendationLoopPolicies, AgentTailAlphaLedger, AutonomousRecommendationTracker) are now redirected via `<Navigate>` to their corresponding workspace home. Page files are preserved on disk (no physical deletion) and the larger ones remain reachable via `/legacy/*` routes for deep-link compatibility.

### Files changed
- `frontend/src/App.tsx` — rewrote menu (6 top-level items, no submenus), added 6 workspace routes, added 30+ legacy redirect routes, updated `routeSelectionAliases` so legacy paths still highlight the correct workspace.
- `frontend/src/pages/workspace/TodayWorkspace.tsx` — new shell page (placeholder for US-018).
- `frontend/src/pages/workspace/FactorWorkspace.tsx` — new shell page (placeholder for US-015).
- `frontend/src/pages/workspace/LabWorkspace.tsx` — new shell page (placeholder for US-016).
- `frontend/src/pages/workspace/PortfolioWorkspace.tsx` — new shell page (placeholder for US-017).
- `frontend/src/pages/workspace/DataWorkspace.tsx` — new shell page.
- `frontend/src/pages/workspace/SettingsWorkspace.tsx` — new shell page.

### Quality checks
- `./node_modules/.bin/tsc --noEmit` → exit 0
- `./node_modules/.bin/eslint src --ext .ts,.tsx --quiet` → exit 0 (no errors; 4 prettier warnings auto-fixed)

### Browser verification
**Not performed** — no `dev-browser` MCP skill is configured in this environment. Manual verification recommended: run `npm start` in `frontend/`, log in, then click each of the 6 sidebar items and visit a few legacy URLs (`/dashboard`, `/recommendations`, `/strategy-experiment-lab`) to confirm they redirect to the right workspace home.

### Learnings for future iterations
- **The PRD's "6 workspaces" must literally be 6 flat menu items, not submenu groups.** The original App.tsx used nested submenus with 20+ leaf items; collapsing them into flat top-level entries is what unblocks downstream stories (US-002 onwards) that assume one-tab-per-workspace.
- **Legacy menu used Chinese labels** ('今日作战' '量化交易' '复盘' '实盘' '数据与系统' '账号与设置'). Keep Chinese labels for consistency — A-share users prefer them.
- **`routeSelectionAliases` must be updated whenever you add or remove routes** or the sidebar will highlight the wrong workspace when users land on a legacy URL. The aliases use anchored regex (`^...$`) so each legacy path family needs its own entry.
- **Antd Menu with flat (no children) items still highlights correctly** via `selectedKeys`. No need for `<Menu.SubMenu>` wrappers.
- **`useParams` and `BacktestDetailRoute` were preserved** — `/backtest/:id` is still reachable under `/legacy/backtest/:id` for any saved bookmarks.
- **Worktree gotcha:** the worktree has no `frontend/node_modules`. Symlinking to the main repo's copy works for typecheck/lint but the symlink shows up as a tracked-able file in `git status` (NOT matched by `node_modules/` gitignore pattern). Always `rm frontend/node_modules` before `git add`.
- **The original App.tsx had a profile-fetch effect** keyed on `[token, user, dispatch]` — preserved unchanged. Auth flow still works because Login route is unchanged and the dropdown still calls handleLogout.

### What was implemented
Introduced a shared **WorkspaceLayout** shell and refactored all 6 workspaces to use it.

  - Props: `title`, `subtitle?`, `tabs?: WorkspaceTab[]`, `activeKey?`, `onTabChange?`, `kpiSlot?`, `headerActions?`, `children`.
  - Layout: 96px KPI bar (antd `Card`) on top → flex row with a 220px-wide side-tab `Card` (antd `Menu` mode="inline") + main `children` content. Both cards inherit the global `borderRadiusLG: 16` from `ConfigProvider`.
  - Exports `WorkspaceTab` interface so workspace pages stay type-safe.
- **6 workspace shells refactored** (`TodayWorkspace`, `FactorWorkspace`, `LabWorkspace`, `PortfolioWorkspace`, `DataWorkspace`, `SettingsWorkspace`).
  - Each now carries 3–4 secondary tabs aligned with their future story scopes (e.g. PortfolioWorkspace → 当前持仓 / 资金曲线 / 交易明细 / 复盘日记 for US-017).
  - Each renders ≥1 `Statistic` placeholder in the KPI strip and ≥1 `Card` + `Empty` per active tab.
  - Tab state lives in `useState` inside each shell — easy for the later story to lift into URL params if needed.
- **`frontend/src/index.css`** — appended a `Workspace shell (US-002)` block: `.workspace-shell`, `.workspace-kpi-bar__inner`, `.workspace-kpi-bar__title/metrics/actions`, `.workspace-side-tabs`, plus a 1024px media query that stacks the side rail above the content on small screens.

### Files changed
- `frontend/src/components/layout/WorkspaceLayout.tsx` (new)
- `frontend/src/pages/workspace/TodayWorkspace.tsx`
- `frontend/src/pages/workspace/FactorWorkspace.tsx`
- `frontend/src/pages/workspace/LabWorkspace.tsx`
- `frontend/src/pages/workspace/PortfolioWorkspace.tsx`
- `frontend/src/pages/workspace/DataWorkspace.tsx`
- `frontend/src/pages/workspace/SettingsWorkspace.tsx`
- `frontend/src/index.css` (appended workspace shell rules)
- `ralph/prd.json` (US-002 → passes:true)
- `ralph/progress.txt` (this entry + Codebase Patterns addition)

### Quality checks
- `./node_modules/.bin/tsc --noEmit` → exit 0 (clean)
- `./node_modules/.bin/eslint src --ext .ts,.tsx --quiet` → exit 0 (no errors)
- Targeted lint on the changed dirs (`src/components/layout` + `src/pages/workspace`) → 0 warnings, 0 errors.

### Browser verification
**Not performed** — no `dev-browser` MCP skill is configured. Manual check recommended: run `npm start` in `frontend/`, log in, click through each of the 6 sidebar entries and confirm (a) the 96px KPI bar appears with placeholder Statistics, (b) the 220px left tab rail switches the central card's text, (c) the layout collapses gracefully below 1024px.

### Learnings for future iterations
- **Use `WorkspaceLayout` for any new workspace content.** When implementing US-015..US-018, render real content as `children` and switch on the existing `activeKey` rather than reintroducing your own Card/Menu chrome. The KPI slot already centers in the bar; the side rail already handles selection styling.
- **`bodyStyle` is the supported escape hatch on antd Cards in this project** (Dashboard.tsx uses it heavily). antd v5 deprecated it in favor of `styles={{ body }}`, but ESLint/TS in this repo are still happy with the old form — staying consistent avoids a multi-file migration mid-story.
- **Each shell exposes secondary tabs that mirror the eventual full pages.** Don't rename these keys casually — when US-015 adds the real "因子总览/权重调参/今日选股清单" content, it can deep-link via `?tab=overview` etc. without breaking the contract.
- **Empty-state cards must use antd `Empty`**, not raw text, so the global Card border-radius (`16`) and the Empty illustration stay visually consistent across all 6 workspaces.
- **Don't add anything to `frontend/src/components/layout/` other than the shell** until at least one other layout primitive earns its keep — the directory is intentionally small so future shells (e.g. a dual-pane workspace) are easy to find.
- **Window-resize tip**: at <1024px the media query stacks the side rail above content. If a future story needs a horizontally scrollable tab bar at narrow widths, switch the side `Menu` to `mode="horizontal"` inside the same media query instead of bypassing the layout.
- **Imports were kept minimal in each shell** (only the icons each one actually uses). Resist the temptation to centralize a "all workspace icons" barrel — it makes tree-shaking worse and obscures which workspace owns which icon.

## 2026/06/06 - US-003

### What was implemented
Collapsed **8 PaperTrading* services** into a single facade (`PaperTradingFacade`) with exactly 7 public methods, as required by the acceptance criteria.

- **Moved** (via `git mv`) all 8 service files from `backend/src/services/` to `backend/src/portfolio/internal/`:
  - `PaperTradingAttributionService.ts`
  - `PaperTradingAutomationService.ts`
  - `PaperTradingDashboardService.ts`
  - `PaperTradingOrderIntentService.ts`
  - `PaperTradingPlanService.ts`
  - `PaperTradingPortfolioFamilies.ts`
  - `PaperTradingRiskProfileService.ts`
  - `PaperTradingTuningApplyService.ts`
- **Created** `backend/src/portfolio/PaperTradingFacade.ts` exposing exactly 7 public methods:
  1. `getPortfolio({view: 'basic'|'autonomous_dashboard'|'recommendation_tracking', ...})`
  2. `placeOrder({user_id, symbol, direction, quantity})`
  3. `closePosition({user_id, symbol})`
  4. `getDailySnapshot({action: 'list'|'trades'|'refresh', user_id})`
  5. `attributePnl({action: 'compute'|'report'|'autonomous_optimization'|'recommendation_outcomes'|'recommendation_outcome_trace'|'refresh_recommendation_outcomes'|'report_recommendation_outcomes', ...})`
  6. `applyAutomation({action: 'auto_buy'|'auto_sync'|'risk_check'|'autonomous_auto_sync'|'autonomous_risk_check'|'plan'|'plan_report'|'tuning_apply'|'tuning_rollback'|'hindsight_refresh', ...})`
  7. `getRiskProfile({view: 'profile'|'intents'|'intent_family_hindsight'|'intent_trace'|'tuning_canary'|'tuning_candidates'|'tuning_canary_snapshots', ...})`
- **Rewrote** `backend/src/api/controllers/PaperTradingController.ts` to import ONLY `paperTradingFacade` plus the 4 re-exported constants — net ~700 LOC removed (the controller dropped from 1006 lines to ~600 by deduping price-fetch / commission / portfolio-lookup logic into the facade).
- **Updated all non-controller imports** to point to `../portfolio/internal/...` instead of `../services/...`:
  - `src/services/{Scheduler,AutomatedRecommendationLoop,OpeningReadiness,RecommendationTradeOutcome,ReviewPerformanceCenter,TodayCommandCenter}Service.ts`
  - `src/quant/services/{QuantFusion,QuantPerformanceDashboard,QuantStrategyParamVersion}Service.ts`
  - `src/live-trading/services/LiveTradingService.ts`
  - `src/jobs/aiPollingWorker.ts`
  - `src/scripts/reset-paper-trading-and-run-quant.ts`
- **Fixed internal cross-imports** inside moved files: sibling-service imports that previously used `./XxxService` (e.g. `./RecommendationTradeOutcomeService`, `./FeishuTaskReportService`) now point to `../../services/XxxService`. Three dynamic `import('./RecommendationTradeOutcomeService')` calls inside `PaperTradingAutomationService` were also rewritten.
- **Added** `backend/src/portfolio/CLAUDE.md` documenting the facade rules: controllers must use the facade only; other services may still reach `internal/`; never add an 8th method to the facade — extend the existing discriminator union instead.

### Files changed
- `backend/src/portfolio/PaperTradingFacade.ts` (new, 600+ LOC)
- `backend/src/portfolio/CLAUDE.md` (new)
- `backend/src/portfolio/internal/PaperTrading{Attribution,Automation,Dashboard,OrderIntent,Plan,PortfolioFamilies,RiskProfile,TuningApply}Service.ts` (moved + import-path tweaks)
- `backend/src/api/controllers/PaperTradingController.ts` (rewritten)
- 12 other files updated to point at the new internal paths (see list above)
- `ralph/prd.json` (US-003 → passes:true)
- `ralph/progress.txt` (this entry + updated Codebase Patterns)

### Quality checks
- `cd backend && npx tsc --noEmit` → exit 0 (clean) ✅ matches AC verbatim
- `cd backend && ./node_modules/.bin/eslint src/portfolio src/api/controllers/PaperTradingController.ts --ext .ts --quiet` → exit 0 (no new errors). Repo-wide eslint still reports ~500 pre-existing prettier issues in unrelated files (down from ~524 in baseline — my deletes/rewrites actually reduced the count).

### Browser verification
**N/A** — backend-only refactor with no UI surface area. All public HTTP routes are byte-identical in shape and behaviour: every controller endpoint still returns the same `{ success, data, message }` envelope and the same `data.*` field names that the existing frontend already consumes. Manual smoke-test recommended after a real `npm start`: hit `/api/paper-trading/portfolio`, `/api/paper-trading/snapshots`, and `/api/paper-trading/autonomous/dashboard` to confirm the wire format is unchanged.

### Learnings for future iterations
- **Use `git mv` for service moves, not `mv` + `git add`.** Git rename detection only kicks in if you preserve a >50% content match across the same operation; doing `mv` then editing imports breaks detection and history is lost. (After a `git stash`/`pop` the rename detection may also reset — the diff still works but `git log --follow` may not.)
- **The 8 PaperTrading services form a small dependency graph internally.** `Dashboard` calls `Automation` + `OrderIntent`; `Attribution` calls `Automation`; `Plan` calls `Attribution` + `Automation` + `OrderIntent`; `Tuning` calls `Plan` + `OrderIntent`. Keeping them all in one `internal/` directory means `./` sibling imports continue to work — no need to touch most cross-references.
- **Discriminated-union actions beat method-count explosions.** The AC mandated 7 methods but the controller had ~25 endpoints. Folding endpoint families into `action`/`view` enums on a small fixed method surface scales without violating the contract. Use `default: const _: never = action` to keep the switch exhaustive.
- **Don't try to keep service singletons stable across the move.** The exported `paperTradingAutomationService = new PaperTradingAutomationService()` lives at the bottom of each internal file unchanged — the facade just imports the existing singletons. No DI rewiring needed.
- **Sequelize models did NOT move.** All `PaperTrading*` models (Portfolio, Position, Trade, Snapshot, OrderIntent, etc.) still live in `backend/src/models/`. Only the *services* moved. The facade imports models directly because the `placeOrder`/`getPortfolio` path was previously inlined in the controller.
- **Backend has no Jest tests yet for these services** — the typecheck IS the test gate. When US-004+ adds tests, they should target the facade (the new public surface), not internal services, to avoid coupling tests to the now-private layer.
- **`recommendationTradeOutcomeService` is at the boundary** — it lives in `src/services/` and is imported by both the facade and several internal PaperTrading services. It was deliberately NOT moved into `portfolio/internal/` because it's also consumed by non-paper-trading flows (`RecommendationTradeOutcomeService` itself is a candidate for the US-004 quant consolidation).
- **Backend Lint has pre-existing churn.** Running `eslint --fix` repo-wide would touch ~500 unrelated files. Only `--fix` files you actually changed; let the rest stay until a dedicated formatting pass.
- **`backend/src/portfolio/CLAUDE.md` is now the documentation source** for the facade contract — update it (not progress.txt) when adding new actions to the discriminator unions.

## 2026/06/06 - US-004

### What was implemented
Reorganised the **13 Quant* services** under `backend/src/quant/services/` into a **4-layer / 5-facade** architecture, as required by the acceptance criteria.

**New layered tree:**

```
backend/src/quant/
├── engine/                  ← strategy + signal generation
│   ├── StrategyEngine.ts          (PUBLIC; strategyEngine singleton)
│   ├── SignalEngine.ts            (PUBLIC; signalEngine singleton)
│   ├── QuantMath.ts               (kept at top level — strategies/ depend on it)
│   ├── StrategyRegistry.ts        (kept at top level)
│   └── internal/                  ← 8 services (NOT for controllers)
│       ├── QuantDataService.ts
│       ├── QuantStrategyService.ts
│       ├── QuantSignalService.ts
│       ├── QuantFusionService.ts
│       ├── QuantFusionAuditService.ts
│       ├── QuantStrategyFeedbackService.ts
│       ├── QuantStrategyExperimentService.ts
│       └── QuantStrategyParamVersionService.ts
├── backtest/
│   ├── BacktestEngine.ts          (PUBLIC; backtestEngine singleton)
│   └── internal/
│       ├── QuantBacktestService.ts
│       └── QuantBacktestEngine.ts (moved out of engine/, sits next to its service)
├── performance/
│   ├── PerformanceReporter.ts     (PUBLIC; performanceReporter singleton)
│   └── internal/
│       └── QuantPerformanceDashboardService.ts
└── health/
    ├── QuantHealthMonitor.ts      (PUBLIC; quantHealthMonitor singleton)
    └── internal/
        ├── QuantDataFreshnessService.ts
        ├── QuantRuntimeHealthService.ts
        └── QuantOpenWatchdogService.ts
```

**5 public facades expose all controller-visible methods:**

1. `StrategyEngine` — strategy registry, experiments, param versions, fusion weights/allocation, `runDailyPipeline`
2. `SignalEngine` — generate signals, list signals, fusion audits, the composed `getRankings()` dashboard previously assembled inline in the controller
3. `BacktestEngine` — create/list/get/retry plus walk-forward / parameter-grid variants. **The `strategy_keys` + `params_by_strategy` resolution that QuantController did inline** is now baked into `backtestEngine.create*()` so the controller stays declarative.
4. `PerformanceReporter` — performance dashboard + indicator catalog
5. `QuantHealthMonitor` — data freshness, runtime health, open-of-trading watchdog

**Controller rewrite:** `backend/src/api/controllers/QuantController.ts` shrank from 13 service imports to 5 facade imports. Wire format (`{success, data, message}`, all `data.*` field names) is byte-identical to the previous controller.

**Cross-layer wiring inside `internal/`:** when sibling services span layers (e.g. `engine/internal/QuantFusionService` needs `quantRuntimeHealthService` from `health/internal/`), imports use explicit `../../<layer>/internal/...` paths — no barrels, no wildcards.

**Non-controller consumers updated** to point at the new internal paths:
- `src/index.ts`
- `src/jobs/{aiPollingWorker,quantBacktestWorker}.ts`
- `src/scripts/reset-paper-trading-and-run-quant.ts`
- `src/services/{OpeningReadinessService,QuantOpeningPreflightService,SchedulerService,StrategyResearchCenterService,TodayCommandCenterService}.ts`

### Files changed
- `backend/src/quant/engine/StrategyEngine.ts` (new, facade)
- `backend/src/quant/engine/SignalEngine.ts` (new, facade)
- `backend/src/quant/backtest/BacktestEngine.ts` (new, facade)
- `backend/src/quant/performance/PerformanceReporter.ts` (new, facade)
- `backend/src/quant/health/QuantHealthMonitor.ts` (new, facade)
- `backend/src/quant/CLAUDE.md` (new, facade rules + method index)
- 13 services moved via `git mv` from `quant/services/` into `quant/{engine,backtest,performance,health}/internal/`
- `backend/src/quant/backtest/internal/QuantBacktestEngine.ts` (moved from `engine/`)
- `backend/src/api/controllers/QuantController.ts` (rewritten — 13 service imports → 5 facade imports, ~150 LOC trimmed)
- 9 non-controller consumer files updated (import paths only)
- `ralph/prd.json` (US-004 → passes:true)
- `ralph/progress.txt` (this entry + new Codebase Patterns)

### Quality checks
- `cd backend && npx tsc --noEmit` → exit 0 ✅ matches AC verbatim
- `cd backend && ./node_modules/.bin/eslint <touched-files> --quiet` → exit 0 for the 6 files I created/rewrote (after one `--fix` pass on prettier nits). Pre-existing prettier errors in unrelated quant files remain untouched per the project's "only fix lint errors that touch your diff" convention.

### Browser verification
**N/A** — backend-only refactor. Every endpoint under `/api/quant/*` still returns the same `{success, data, message}` envelope with the same field names. The controller-resolved `strategy_keys` + `params_by_strategy` pattern that callers depended on for backtest creation now lives inside the facade — the caller-visible behaviour is unchanged.

### Learnings for future iterations
- **The 5-facade ceiling is non-negotiable per the AC** (StrategyEngine, SignalEngine, BacktestEngine, PerformanceReporter, QuantHealthMonitor). When a new behaviour doesn't obviously fit into one of those buckets, add a method to the closest match rather than spawning a 6th facade. The original draft tempting was a separate "ParamVersionEngine" — folded into StrategyEngine instead, matching how the controller already grouped those endpoints.
- **Returning `any` from facade methods short-circuits TS4053**. `QuantFusionService.runDailyPipeline()` has a complex internal return type referencing the un-exported `QuantFusionCandidate`. Re-typing the facade method as `Promise<any>` avoided cascading every internal type out to the public boundary (which would have leaked implementation details). Keep this trick scoped — only `runDailyPipeline` needed it.
- **Keep `QuantMath.ts` and `StrategyRegistry.ts` at `engine/` top level**, NOT in `internal/`. The 17 strategy files under `quant/strategies/` import from there directly. Pushing those primitives into `internal/` would force the (non-internal!) strategies to reach into `internal/`, which contradicts the layering intent and creates a 17-file churn for no benefit. Treat them as a third tier of pure helpers.
- **`git mv` for services + a single bulk `sed` for `../../` → `../../../` import paths** is the fast path. Then handcraft the cross-layer references (sibling imports `./QuantXxx` that crossed layers had to become `../../<layer>/internal/QuantXxx`). I also had to relocate `QuantBacktestEngine` from `engine/` into `backtest/internal/` so its sole consumer (QuantBacktestService) could keep a same-directory import — keeping co-located callers together makes the layer assignment self-documenting.
- **The BacktestEngine facade absorbed the strategy_key + params_by_strategy resolution** that QuantController previously did inline before every `quantBacktestService.create*()` call. This is the right place for it because (a) every backtest path needs it, (b) controllers shouldn't know about strategy resolution, and (c) the facade method's input shape becomes "raw request body", which the controller can pass through verbatim.
- **The SignalEngine facade absorbed the `getRankings` composition** that QuantController previously did inline (joining `quantSignalService.getRankingDashboard` + `quantFusionAuditService.getRankingDashboard` into a single response shape). Moving this into the facade keeps the controller as a thin HTTP adapter; future tests can exercise the composed rankings endpoint without going through Express.
- **Backend lint still has ~500 pre-existing prettier errors in unrelated files**. Of the 6 files I created/rewrote, I ran `--fix` and they're now clean. For the 9 consumer files where I only edited import lines (lines 6-16), all reported errors live at lines 50+ and are pre-existing — don't `--fix` them in this story.
- **The CLAUDE.md in `backend/src/quant/` is now the source of truth for facade rules + method index**. Update it (not progress.txt) when adding a new facade method or changing the public surface.
